v1868:Button("\229\175\187\230\137\190\232\128\131\230\139\137\229\176\143\229\173\144", function()
    local v1886, v1887, v1888 = pairs(workspace:GetChildren())
    local v1889 = false
    while true do
        local v1890
        v1888, v1890 = v1886(v1887, v1888)
        if v1888 == nil then
            break
        end
        if v1890:IsA("Model") and v1890.Name:lower() == "koalakid" then
            local v1891 = v1890:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or v1890.PrimaryPart
            if v1891 then
                v1889 = safeTeleport(v1891.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, 2))
                break
            end
        end
    end
    if v1889 then
        WindUI:Notify({
            Title = "\228\188\160\233\128\129\230\136\144\229\138\159",
            Text = "\229\183\178\228\188\160\233\128\129\232\135\179\232\128\131\230\139\137\229\176\143\229\173\144",
            Duration = 3
        })
    else
        WindUI:Notify({
            Title = "\230\156\170\230\137\190\229\136\176",
            Text = "\232\128\131\230\139\137\229\176\143\229\173\144\230\156\170\229\136\183\230\150\176\230\136\150\228\184\141\229\143\175\232\190\190",
            Duration = 3
        })
    end
end)
v1868:Button("\228\188\160\233\128\129\229\155\158\231\129\171\230\151\129", function()
    game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(- 0.09672313928604126, 7.937822341918945, - 0.1782056838274002)
end)
function teleportItemsToPlayer(p1892)
    local v1893 = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character
    if v1893 then
        local v1894 = v1893:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if v1894 then
            local v1895, v1896, v1897 = pairs(workspace:GetDescendants())
            local v1898 = {}
            while true do
                local v1899
                v1897, v1899 = v1895(v1896, v1897)
                if v1897 == nil then
                    break
                end
                if v1899.Name == p1892 then
                    table.insert(v1898, v1899)
                end
            end
            local v1900, v1901, v1902 = ipairs(v1898)
            while true do
                local v1903
                v1902, v1903 = v1900(v1901, v1902)
                if v1902 == nil then
                    break
                end
                if v1903:IsA("BasePart") then
                    v1903.CFrame = v1894.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 0, - 2)
                elseif v1903:IsA("Model") then
                    local v1904 = v1903.PrimaryPart or v1903:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
                    if v1904 then
                        v1904.CFrame = v1894.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 0, - 2)
                    end
                end
            end
            return # v1898
        end
    end
end
function safeTeleport(p1905)
    local v1906 = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
    local v1907 = (v1906.Character or v1906.CharacterAdded:Wait()):WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not p1905 or p1905.Position.Magnitude <= 0 then
        return false
    end
    v1907.CFrame = p1905 + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)
    return true
end
function fullMapWeaponAttack(p1908)
    if game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character then
        if game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("ToolHandle") then
            local v1909 = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.ToolHandle.OriginalItem.Value
            if v1909 and v1909.Name == p1908 then
                local v1910, v1911, v1912 = pairs(workspace.Characters:GetChildren())
                while true do
                    local v1913
                    v1912, v1913 = v1910(v1911, v1912)
                    if v1912 == nil then
                        break
                    end
                    if v1913:IsA("Model") and (v1913:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and v1913:FindFirstChild("HitRegisters")) then
                        game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):WaitForChild("RemoteEvents"):WaitForChild("ToolDamageObject"):InvokeServer(v1913, v1909, true, game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame)
                    end
                end
            end
        else
            return
        end
    else
        return
    end
end
function collectAllItems(p1914)
    if p1914 == "Old Sack" and autoCollectEnabledOldSack or autoCollectEnabledGoodSack then
        local v1915 = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
        if v1915.Character and v1915.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
            local v1916 = v1915.Character.HumanoidRootPart
            local v1917 = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").TempStorage
            local v1918, v1919, v1920 = pairs(workspace.Items:GetChildren())
            while true do
                local v1921
                v1920, v1921 = v1918(v1919, v1920)
                if v1920 == nil then
                    break
                end
                if v1921:IsA("Model") then
                    local v1922 = v1921.PrimaryPart or v1921:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
                    if v1922 and (v1916.Position - v1922.Position).Magnitude <= collectionRange then
                        game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").RemoteEvents.StopDraggingItem:FireServer(v1921)
                        local v1923 = v1915.Inventory:FindFirstChild(p1914)
                        if v1923 then
                            v1921.Parent = v1917
                            game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").RemoteEvents.RequestBagStoreItem:InvokeServer(v1923, v1921)
                            game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").RemoteEvents.ReplicateSound:FireServer("FireAllClients", "BagGet", {
                                Instance = v1915.Character.Head,
                                Volume = 0.25
                            })
                            WindUI:Notify({
                                Title = "\232\135\170\229\138\168\230\148\182\233\155\134",
                                Text = "\229\183\178\230\148\182\233\155\134: " .. v1921.Name,
                                Duration = 1
                            })
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    else
        return
    end
end
function CollectCoal()
    if autoCollectCoal then
        local v1924 = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
        if v1924.Character and v1924.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
            local v1925 = v1924.Character.HumanoidRootPart
            local v1926 = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").TempStorage
            local v1927 = v1924.Inventory:FindFirstChild("Old Sack")
            if v1927 then
                local v1928, v1929, v1930 = pairs(workspace.Items:GetChildren())
                while true do
                    local v1931
                    v1930, v1931 = v1928(v1929, v1930)
                    if v1930 == nil then
                        break
                    end
                    if v1931.Name == "Coal" and v1931:IsA("Model") then
                        local v1932 = v1931.PrimaryPart or v1931:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
                        if v1932 and (v1925.Position - v1932.Position).Magnitude <= collectRange then
                            game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").RemoteEvents.ReplicateSound:FireServer("FireAllClients", "BagGet", {
                                Instance = v1924.Character.Head,
                                Volume = 0.25
                            })
                            game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").RemoteEvents.RequestStartDraggingItem:FireServer(v1931)
                            v1931.Parent = v1926
                            game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").RemoteEvents.StopDraggingItem:FireServer(v1931)
                            game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").RemoteEvents.RequestBagStoreItem:InvokeServer(v1927, v1931)
                            task.wait(0.3)
                        end
                    end
                end
            else
                WindUI:Notify({
                    Title = "\233\156\128\232\166\129\232\128\129\232\162\139\229\173\144",
                    Text = "\232\175\183\229\133\136\232\163\133\229\164\135Old Sack",
                    Duration = 3
                })
            end
        else
            return
        end
    else
        return
    end
end

    if autoCollectLogs then

                    local v1940

                        break

                    if v1940.Name == "Log" and v1940:IsA("Model") then
                        local v1941 = v1940.PrimaryPart or v1940:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")

                            game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").RemoteEvents.ReplicateSound:FireServer("FireAllClients", "BagGet", {
                                Instance = v1933.Character.Head,

                            game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").RemoteEvents.RequestBagStoreItem:InvokeServer(v1936, v1940)
                            task.wait(collectDelay)
                        end

                end
            else
                game:GetService("StarterGui"):SetCore("SendNotification", {

                })

        return
    end
end
function EatCarrots()
    if autoEatCarrots then
        local v1942 = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
        if v1942.Character and v1942.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
            local v1943 = v1942.Character.HumanoidRootPart
            local v1944 = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").TempStorage
            local v1945, v1946, v1947 = pairs(workspace.Items:GetChildren())
            while true do
                local v1948
                v1947, v1948 = v1945(v1946, v1947)
                if v1947 == nil then
                    break
                end
                if v1948.Name == "Carrot" and v1948:IsA("Model") then
                    local v1949 = v1948.PrimaryPart or v1948:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
                    if v1949 and (v1943.Position - v1949.Position).Magnitude <= eatRange then
                        game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").RemoteEvents.ReplicateSound:FireServer("FireAllClients", "Eat", {
                            Instance = v1942.Character.Head,
                            Volume = 0.15
                        })
                        v1948.Parent = v1944
                        game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").RemoteEvents.StopDraggingItem:FireServer(v1948)
                        game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").RemoteEvents.RequestConsumeItem:InvokeServer(v1948)
                        task.wait(1)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    else
        return
    end
end
